The cache shipped\n# with the dataset is pre-dated so it survives; this keeps what this run caches alive too.\nREST_EXPIRY = datetime.timedelta(days=365 * 50)\n\nT_START, T_STOP = -0.5, 1.5      # decoding window around stimulus onset, following the reference code\nBIN = 0.02\nN_BINS = int(round((T_STOP - T_START) / BIN))            # 100\nEDGES = T_START + BIN * np.arange(N_BINS + 1)\nTIME = EDGES[:-1] + BIN / 2                              # bin centres, the first decoder input\n\n# reaction time bounds of the paper, which truncates the distribution at 80 ms and 2 s;\nMIN_RT, MAX_RT = 0.08, 2.0\n\nQC_LABEL = 1.0                   # the \"stringent quality control\" of the paper, 75,708 units of 621,733\nSPLIT = [100 / 3, 2 * 100 / 3]       # equal sized classes, from percentiles of each session's own trace\n\nCHOICE = {1.0: 0, -1.0: 1}       # +1 is a leftward choice, -1 rightward; 0 is a no response, dropped\nPRIOR = {0.2: 0, 0.5: 1, 0.8: 2}\nMOVEMENT = ['low', 'medium', 'high']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stimulus_onset', 'trial_number_in_block']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_probability_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [['left', 'right'], ['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'], MOVEMENT, MOVEMENT]\n\n# the cache location is not known until --datadir is parsed, and the connection cannot be\n# shared across a fork anyway, so connect() fills this in per process rather than at import\none = None\nsubset_csv = None    # DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv path, or None on the full dataset; set by connect()\nbrain_regions = BrainRegions()\n\n\ndef connect(datadir):\n    \"\"\"Point the module-level ONE client at the cache under datadir.\n\n    An Alyx-backed client is required: eid2pid, which load_neural() uses to find the probe\n    insertions, exists only on OneAlyx. mode='local' returns a plain One without it. The\n    client also resolves each dataset's current revision, which is what is on disk -- the\n    release tables list the pre-revision paths, so resolving from them alone finds no\n    trials. The metadata that resolution needs is cached under one_cache/.rest, so a run\n    does not have to reach Alyx.\n\n    datadir: directory holding the one_cache/ folder.\n    Sets the globals `one` and `subset_csv`; returns nothing.\n    \"\"\"\n    global one\n    global subset_csv\n    cache = os.path.join(datadir, ONE_CACHE_DIRNAME)\n    tables = os.path.join(cache, RELEASE)\n    client = dict(base_url=BASE_URL, silent=True, cache_dir=cache, tables_dir=tables,\n                  mode='remote')\n    try:\n        # No password: passing one forces re-authentication over the network, while the\n        # token the fetch cached needs no connection.\n        one = ONE(**client)\n    except Exception:\n        one = ONE(password=PASSWORD, **client)\n    one.alyx.default_expiry = REST_EXPIRY\n    # One.load_cache reads the parquet tables where they are; the OneAlyx override would\n    # ask Alyx for a newer copy first. In 'remote' mode nothing is loaded until this is\n    # called, and search() then returns no sessions at all, without raising.\n    One.load_cache(one, tables_dir=tables)\n    # Only the datalimit dataset ships this file; on the full dataset there is nothing to\n    # restrict to, and subset_csv stays None.\n    path = os.path.join(datadir, 'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv')\n    subset_csv = path if os.path.isfile(path) else None\n\n\n# --- sessions ---\n\ndef select_sessions():\n    \"\"\"Released sessions that have everything the decoder needs, grouped by subject.\"\"\"\n\n    required = ['spikes.times.npy', 'spikes.clusters.npy',          # ephys\n                '_ibl_trials.table.pqt',                            # task\n                '_ibl_wheel.position.npy', '_ibl_wheel.timestamps.npy']\n\n    keep = set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=required))\n    keep &= (set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['leftCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                          '_ibl_leftCamera.times.npy']))\n             | set(one.search(query_type='local', datasets=['rightCamera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                                            '_ibl_rightCamera.times.npy'])))\n    # The datalimit dataset ships a subset of the release (see DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv). The ONE\n    # release index is unmodified and still lists every released session, so\n    # restrict to the eids named there -- otherwise a session that is not on disk\n    # would be fetched over the network. The full dataset has no such file, and every\n    # session the index lists is on disk.\n    # Compare in string space: one.search returns UUID objects while the csv column\n    # is str, so a plain set intersection is empty whatever the contents. keep must\n    # stay UUID-valued for the `eid in keep` test below.\n    if subset_csv is not None:\n        subset_eids = set(pd.read_csv(subset_csv).eid.astype(str))\n        keep = {eid for eid in keep if str(eid) in subset_eids}\n        if not keep:\n            raise SystemExit(f'No sessions matched {subset_csv}; refusing to continue.')\n\n    eids, info = one.search(query_type='local', details=True)\n    selected = {}\n    for eid, session in zip(eids, info):\n        if eid in keep:\n            selected.setdefault(session['subject'], []).append(str(eid))\n    return {subject: selected[subject] for subject in sorted(selected)}\n\n\ndef camera_view(eid):\n    \"\"\"The side camera this session has whisker motion energy for, left preferred.\"\"\"\n    for view in ('left', 'right'):\n        if len(one.list_datasets(eid, filename=f'{view}Camera.ROIMotionEnergy.npy',\n                                 query_type='local')):\n            return view\n    raise ValueError(f'no whisker motion energy for {eid}')\n\n\n# --- trials ---\n\ndef trial_variables(trials):\n    \"\"\"Choice, block prior and trial number in block, one row per trial.\"\"\"\n    # probabilityLeft is constant within a block, so a change of value starts a new block\n    block = (trials.probabilityLeft != trials.probabilityLeft.shift()).cumsum()\n\n    return pd.DataFrame({'choice': trials.choice.map(CHOICE),\n                         'prior': trials.probabilityLeft.map(PRIOR),\n                         'trial_in_block': trials.groupby(block).cumcount()})\n\n\ndef covered(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Trials whose window is spanned by this stream, allowing the one bin gap the reference allows.\"\"\"\n    start, stop = stim_on + T_START, stim_on + T_STOP\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, start, side='right')      # first sample inside the window\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, stop, side='left') - 1       # last sample inside the window\n\n    # a trial with no sample at all, or a gap at either edge, has no usable trace\n    inside = begin <= end\n    first = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(begin, 0, times.size - 1)], np.inf)\n    last = np.where(inside, times[np.clip(end, 0, times.size - 1)], -np.inf)\n    return inside & (np.abs(start - first) <= BIN) & (np.abs(stop - last) <= BIN)\n\n\ndef usable_trials(trials, wheel_times, camera_times):\n    \"\"\"Trials the paper would keep, that also have a full wheel and camera window.\"\"\"\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()\n    reaction = trials.firstMovement_times.to_numpy() - stim_on\n\n    keep = (reaction >= MIN_RT) & (reaction <= MAX_RT)          # NaN compares false, so those go too\n    keep &= trials.choice.isin(CHOICE).to_numpy()               # drops the no response trials\n    keep &= trials.probabilityLeft.isin(PRIOR).to_numpy()\n\n    return keep & covered(wheel_times, stim_on) & covered(camera_times, stim_on)\n\n\n# --- behavior ---\n\ndef window_slice(times, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Index range of each trial window, so a stream is sliced rather than rescanned per trial.\"\"\"\n    window_start = stim_on + T_START\n    window_stop = stim_on + T_STOP\n\n    # the samples are in time order, so each window is one contiguous range of them\n    begin = np.searchsorted(times, window_start)\n    end = np.searchsorted(times, window_stop)\n\n    return begin, end\n\n\ndef trial_traces(times, values, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"A continuous trace resampled onto the same time grid for every trial.\"\"\"\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    traces = []\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        # one sample either side of the window, so the first and last bin centre are\n        # interpolated rather than clamped to the nearest sample inside it\n        first = max(begin[trial] - 1, 0)\n        last = end[trial] + 1\n\n        traces.append(np.interp(onset + TIME, times[first:last], values[first:last]))\n\n    return np.array(traces)\n\n\ndef discretize(trace):\n    \"\"\"Equal sized classes, split at percentiles of this session's own trace.\"\"\"\n    return np.digitize(trace, np.percentile(trace, SPLIT))\n\n\n# --- neural ---\n\ndef load_neural(eid):\n    \"\"\"Spike times and good clusters of a session, merged over its probes.\"\"\"\n    times, units, regions = [], [], []\n    for pid, name in zip(*one.eid2pid(eid)):\n        loader = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=name)\n\n        # skip the md5 check, which rereads every spike file and draws its own progress bar\n        spikes, clusters, channels = loader.load_spike_sorting(check_hash=False)\n\n        # a session can hold an insertion whose spike sorting was never released\n        if not clusters:\n            continue\n\n        clusters = loader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels)   # attaches metrics and histology\n\n        # `void` is the atlas label for a channel the histology placed outside the brain,\n        # so a unit there is not in a brain region at all\n        in_brain = brain_regions.acronym2acronym(\n            clusters['acronym'], mapping='Beryl') != 'void'\n\n        # spikes.clusters holds the row of the cluster table, so a per cluster flag\n        # can be indexed by it directly to say whether each spike survives\n        is_good = (clusters['label'] >= QC_LABEL) & in_brain\n        keep = is_good[spikes['clusters']]\n\n        # renumber the surviving clusters, continuing the count from the previous probe\n        new_index = np.cumsum(is_good) - 1\n        units.append(new_index[spikes['clusters'][keep]] + len(regions))\n        times.append(spikes['times'][keep])\n\n        # Beryl is the coarser atlas the reference code uses, e.g. DG-sg and DG-mo both become DG\n        regions.extend(brain_regions.acronym2acronym(clusters['acronym'][is_good], mapping='Beryl'))\n\n    times, units = np.concatenate(times), np.concatenate(units)\n\n    # sort the merged probes by spike time, as the reference does, so trials can be sliced\n    order = np.argsort(times, kind='stable')\n    return times[order], units[order], np.array(regions)\n\n\ndef spike_counts(times, units, n_units, stim_on):\n    \"\"\"Spike count per unit and bin, for every trial.\"\"\"\n    counts = np.empty((stim_on.size, n_units, N_BINS))\n    begin, end = window_slice(times, stim_on)\n\n    for trial, onset in enumerate(stim_on):\n        spike_time = times[begin[trial]:end[trial]] - onset      # time from stimulus onset\n        spike_unit = units[begin[trial]:end[trial]]\n\n        bin_index = np.floor((spike_time - T_START) / BIN).astype(np.int64)\n        \n        # a spike sitting on the window edge can round out of range once the onset is subtracted\n        bin_index = np.clip(bin_index, 0, N_BINS - 1)\n\n        # one flat index per spike lets a single bincount fill the whole unit by bin grid\n        flat_index = spike_unit * N_BINS + bin_index\n        counts[trial] = np.bincount(flat_index, minlength=n_units * N_BINS).reshape(n_units, N_BINS)\n\n    return counts\n\n\n# --- session ---\n\ndef process_session(eid, subject):\n    \"\"\"One session as lists of per trial neural, input and output arrays.\"\"\"\n    loader = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    loader.load_trials()\n    loader.load_wheel()\n    view = camera_view(eid)\n    loader.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n\n    trials, wheel = loader.trials, loader.wheel\n    motion_energy = loader.motion_energy[f'{view}Camera']\n\n    variables = trial_variables(trials)                 # before filtering, so the block count is real\n    keep = usable_trials(trials, wheel.times.to_numpy(), motion_energy.times.to_numpy())\n    variables = variables[keep]\n    stim_on = trials.stimOn_times.to_numpy()[keep]\n\n    # Building ONE with an explicit tables_dir instead uses the\n    73→    frozen release tables, which do NOT list the revisioned datasets that are actually on\n    74→    disk (e.g. alf/#2025-03-03#/_ibl_trials.table.pqt) and silently returns a nearly empty\n    75→    trials table.\n    76→    \"\"\"\n    77→    from one.api import ONE\n    78→    return ONE()\n    79→\n    80→\n    81→def build_trials_mask(trials):\n    82→    \"\"\"Reproduce reference ibl_data_utils.load_trials_and_mask(max_trial_len=10.).\n    83→\n    84→    Returns a boolean numpy array, True for trials to KEEP.\n    85→    \"\"\"\n    86→    query = f'(firstMovement_times - stimOn_times < {MIN_RT})'\n    87→    query += f' | (firstMovement_times - stimOn_times > {MAX_RT})'\n    88→    query += f' | (feedback_times - goCue_times > {MAX_TRIAL_LEN})'\n    89→    for event in NAN_EXCLUDE:\n    90→        query += f' | {event}.isnull()'\n    91→    query += ' | (choice == 0)'\n    92→    return np.asarray(~trials.eval(query), dtype=bool).copy()\n    93→\n    94→\n    95→def trial_number_in_block(prob_left):\n    96→    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its block of constant probabilityLeft.\"\"\"\n    97→    n = len(prob_left)\n    98→    if n == 0:\n    99→        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)\n   100→    is_new = np.empty(n, dtype=bool)\n   101→    is_new[0] = True\n   102→    # NaN != NaN, so a NaN run would be split; treat NaNs as their own value.\n   103→    prev, cur = prob_left[:-1], prob_left[1:]\n   104→    is_new[1:] = ~((prev == cur) | (np.isnan(prev) & np.isnan(cur)))\n   105→    block_start = np.maximum.accumulate(np.where(is_new, np.arange(n), 0))\n   106→    return np.arange(n) - block_start\n   107→\n   108→\n   109→def bin_spikes_trials(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, t_beg):\n   110→    \"\"\"Spike counts per (trial, cluster, bin).\n   111→\n   112→    Equivalent to the reference bincount2D(times, clusters, xbin=BINSIZE,\n   113→    xlim=[t_beg, t_end]) truncated to NBINS bins, but computed for all trials at once.\n   114→\n   115→    Bin i of trial k covers [t_beg[k] + i*BINSIZE, t_beg[k] + (i+1)*BINSIZE).\n   116→\n   117→    Args:\n   118→        spike_times:    (n_spikes,) sorted spike times, seconds\n   119→        spike_clusters: (n_spikes,) cluster index in [0, n_clusters)\n   120→        n_clusters:     number of clusters\n   121→        t_beg:          (n_trials,) window start of each trial, seconds\n   122→\n   123→    Returns:\n   124→        (n_trials, n_clusters, NBINS) float32 array of counts\n   125→    \"\"\"\n   126→    n_trials = len(t_beg)\n   127→    out = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   128→    if n_trials == 0 or len(spike_times) == 0:\n   129→        return out\n   130→\n   131→    t_end = t_beg + NBINS * BINSIZE\n   132→    # (NBINS,)\n   162→    xi = t_beg[:, None] + grid[None, :]                          # (n_trials, NBINS)\n   163→    ok = np.isfinite(t_beg)\n   164→    if len(times) < 2:\n   165→        return np.zeros((n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32), np.zeros(n_trials, bool)\n   166→\n   167→    # degenerate (e.g. a constant trace) -- fall back to unique-value splits\n   191→        uq = np.unique(pool)\n   192→        if uq.size >= 3:\n   193→            lo, hi = uq[len(uq) // 3], uq[2 * len(uq) // 3]\n   194→        elif uq.size == 2:\n   195→            lo = hi = uq[0]\n   196→        else:\n   197→            lo = hi = uq[0]\n   198→    cls = (values > lo).astype(np.int64) + (values > hi).astype(np.int64)\n   199→    return cls, (float(lo), float(hi))\n   200→\n   201→\n   202→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   203→# Per-session conversion\n   204→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   205→def load_session(eid, probes, show_processing=False):\n   206→    \"\"\"Load, curate, align and bin one session.\n   207→\n   208→    Args:\n   209→        eid:     session UUID (str)\n   210→        probes:  DataFrame rows of bwm_release.csv for this eid (pid, probe_name)\n   211→\n   212→    Returns a dict with the per-session arrays, or a dict with 'skip' set.\n   213→    \"\"\"\n   214→    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader, SpikeSortingLoader\n   215→    from iblatlas.regions import BrainRegions\n   216→\n   217→    timings = {}\n   218→    t_start = time.time()\n   219→    one = get_one()\n   220→    br = BrainRegions()\n   221→\n   222→    # Spike-sorting coverage.  The reference applies a coverage test to the behavioural\n   315→    # traces (get_behavior_per_interval rejects an interval whose trace starts too late\n   316→    # or ends too early) but not to the spikes, so a trial lying in a recording drop-out\n   317→    # or past the end of the spike-sorted data silently becomes an all-zero matrix.\n   318→    # Two real cases in this release: session 8c2f7f4d has three trials that start after\n   319→    # its last spike, and session b182b754 has one trial inside a 2.1 s gap.  Apply the\n   320→    # same rule to the neural stream.\n   321→    spk_ok = np.zeros(len(t_beg), dtype=bool)\n   322→    if len(spk_times_k) > 1:\n   323→        t_end_all = t_beg + NBINS * BINSIZE\n   324→        inside = np.isfinite(t_beg) & (t_beg >= spk_times_k[0] - BINSIZE) & \\\n   325→                 (t_end_all <= spk_times_k[-1] + BINSIZE)\n   326→        # a window must not fall inside a gap longer than the window itself\n   327→        i0 = np.searchsorted(spk_times_k, np.where(inside, t_beg, spk_times_k[0]), 'left')\n   328→        i1 = np.searchsorted(spk_times_k, np.where(inside, t_end_all, spk_times_k[0]), 'left')\n   329→        spk_ok = inside & (i1 > i0)\n   330→    trial_mask &= spk_ok\n   331→\n   332→    ws_vals, ws_ok = interp_behavior(wheel_t, wheel_v, t_beg)\n   333→    wm_vals, wm_ok = interp_behavior(whisker_t, whisker_v, t_beg)\n   334→    # reference align_spike_behavior: a trial survives only if it is good in the trials\n   335→    # mask AND in every behaviour mask\n   336→    keep_trial = trial_mask & ws_ok & wm_ok\n   337→    if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n   338→        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': f'only {int(keep_trial.sum())} usable trials'}\n   339→\n   340→    # ---------------- discretise continuous outputs -------------------------------\n   341→    ws_cls, ws_thr = tertile_bins(ws_vals, keep_trial)\n   342→    wm_cls, wm_thr = tertile_bins(wm_vals, keep_trial)\n   343→\n   344→    # ---------------- per-trial variables -----------------------------------------\n   345→    choice = trials['choice'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n   346→    # choice == +1 is a LEFT report, choice == -1 a RIGHT report (verified against\n   347→    # contrastLeft/contrastRight and feedbackType).  Required coding: left = 0, right = 1.\n   348→    choice_cls = (choice < 0).astype(np.int64)\n   349→\n   350→    p_left = trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n   351→    prior_cls = np.full(len(p_left), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   352→    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.2)] = 0\n   353→    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.5)] = 1\n   354→    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.8)] = 2\n   355→    keep_trial = keep_trial & (prior_cls >= 0)\n   356→    if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n   357→        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no trials with a valid probabilityLeft'}\n   358→\n   359→    tib = trial_number_in_block(p_left).astype(np.float32) / TRIAL_IN_BLOCK_SCALE\n   360→\n   361→    # ---------------- bin spikes (kept trials only) --------------------------------\n   362→    t0 = time.time()\n   363→    kt = np.flatnonzero(keep_trial)\n   364→    binned = bin_spikes_trials(spk_times_k, spk_clusters_k, n_neurons, t_beg[kt])\n   365→    timings['binning'] = time.time() - t0\n   366→\n   367→    # ---------------- assemble -----------------------------------------------------\n   368→    time_axis = (TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * np.arange(1, NBINS + 1)).astype(np.float32)\n   369→\n   370→    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n   371→    for j, k in enumerate(kt):\n   372→        neural.append(binned[j])\n   373→        inp = np.empty((2, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n   374→        inp[0] = time_axis\n   375→        inp[1] = tib[k]\n   376→        inputs.append(inp)\n   377→        out = np.empty((4, NBINS), dtype=np.int64)\n   378→        out[0] = choice_cls[k]\n   379→        out[1] = prior_cls[k]\n   380→        out[2] = ws_cls[k]\n   381→        out[3] = wm_cls[k]\n   382→        outputs.append(out)\n   383→\n   384→    res = {\n   385→        'eid': eid,\n   386→        'skip': None,\n   387→        'neural': neural,\n   388→        'input': inputs,\n   389→        'output': outputs,\n   390→        'regions': unit_regions,\n   391→        'n_units_all': n_units_all,\n   392→        'n_good': n_good,\n   393→        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n   394→        'n_trials_raw': int(len(trials)),\n   395→        'n_trials_mask': int(trial_mask.sum()),\n   396→        'n_trials_kept': int(len(kt)),\n   397→        'kept_trial_idx': kt.astype(np.int32),\n   398→        'whisker_view': whisker_view,\n   399→        'wheel_thresholds': ws_thr,\n   400→        'whisker_thresholds': wm_thr,\n   401→        'timings': timings,\n   402→        'total_time': time.time() - t_start,\n   403→    }\n   404→    if show_processing:\n   405→        res['_plot'] = {\n   406→            'trials': trials, 'trial_mask': trial_mask, 'keep_trial': keep_trial,\n   407→            'kt': kt, 't_beg': t_beg, 'time_axis': time_axis,\n   408→            'wheel_t': wheel_t, 'wheel_v': wheel_v,\n   409→            'whisker_t': whisker_t, 'whisker_v': whisker_v,\n   410→            'ws_vals': ws_vals, 'wm_vals': wm_vals,\n   411→            'ws_cls': ws_cls, 'wm_cls': wm_cls,\n   412→            'ws_thr': ws_thr, 'wm_thr': wm_thr,\n   413→            'binned': binned, 'spk_times_k': spk_times_k, 'spk_clusters_k': spk_clusters_k,\n   414→            'regions': unit_regions, 'p_left': p_left, 'tib': tib,\n   415→            'choice_cls': choice_cls, 'prior_cls': prior_cls,\n   416→        }\n   417→    return res\n   418→\n   419→\n   420→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   421→# Diagnostics plot -- one figure per session showing every processing step\n   422→# ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 3])\n   501→    ax.plot(time_axis, np.zeros_like(time_axis) + 0.5, 'k.')\n   502→    ax.plot(time_axis, time_axis, 'b.-', ms=3)\n   503→    ax.axvline(0, color='r');    ax.set_title('output[3] whisker ME class'); ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n   595→\n   596→    fig.suptitle(f\"session {res['eid']}  |  {res['n_neurons']} neurons, \"\n   597→                 f\"{res['n_trials_kept']} trials, T={NBINS}, bin={BINSIZE*1000:.0f} ms, \"\n   598→                 f\"aligned to {ALIGN_TIME} in {TIME_WINDOW}\", fontsize=13)\n   599→    fig.savefig(outfile, --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   605→# Parallel driver\n   606→# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   607→def _worker(task):\n   608→    eid, probes, show = task\n   609→    try:\n   610→        res = load_session(eid, probes, show_processing=show)\n   611→    except Exception as exc:\n   612→        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': f'EXCEPTION {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}',\n   613→                'traceback': traceback.format_exc()}\n   614→    if show and res.get('skip') is None:\n   615→        try:\n   616→            make_processing_plot(res, f'/app/processing_{eid}.png')\n   617→        except Exception:\n   618→            traceback.print_exc()\n   619→    res.pop('_plot', None)\n   620→    return res\n   621→\n   622→\n   623→def main():\n   624→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   625→    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str, help='output pickle path')\n   626→    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n   627→    g.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True,\n   628→                   help='process all sessions (default)')\n   629→    g.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true',\n   630→                   help='process only 2 sessions, for testing')\n   631→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n   632→                    help='save a per-session diagnostics figure for up to 2 sessions')\n   633→    ap.add_argument('--n-workers', type=int, default=24)\n   634→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   635→\n   636→    t_all = time.time()\n   637→\n   638→    bwm = pd.read_csv(BWM_RELEASE_CSV, index_col=0)\n   639→    print(f'bwm_release.csv: {len(bwm)} insertions, {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions, '\n   640→          f'{bwm.subject.nunique()} subjects, {bwm.lab.nunique()} labs')\n   641→\n   642→    # optional data-limited subset (absent on the full dataset)\n   643→    subset_file = '/app/data/DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv'\n   644→    if os.path.exists(subset_file):\n   645→        sub = pd.read_csv(subset_file)\n   646→        col = 'eid' if 'eid' in sub.columns else sub.columns[0]\n   647→        bwm = bwm[bwm.eid.isin(sub[col].astype(str))]\n   648→        print(f'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv found: restricted to {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions')\n   649→\n   650→    eids = list(dict.fromkeys(bwm.eid.tolist()))   # preserve file order, unique\n   651→    if args.sample:\n   652→        eids = eids[:2]\n   653→    n_show = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n   654→    print(f'Processing {len(eids)} sessions with {args.n_workers} workers '\n   655→          f'(show-processing for the first {n_show})')\n   656→\n   657→    by_eid = {e: list(g[['pid', 'probe_name']].itertuples(index=False, name=None))\n   658→              for e, g in bwm.groupby('eid')}\n   659→    subj_of = dict(zip(bwm.eid, bwm.subject))\n   660→    lab_of = dict(zip(bwm.eid, bwm.lab))\n   661→\n   662→    tasks = [(e, by_eid[e], i < n_show) for i, e in enumerate(eids)]\n   663→\n   664→    results = {}\n   665→    done = 0\n   666→    t_loop = time.time()\n   667→    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.n_workers) as ex:\n   668→        futs = {ex.submit(_worker, t): t[0] for t in tasks}\n   669→        for fut in as_completed(futs):\n   670→            r = fut.result()\n   671→            results[r['eid']] = r\n   672→            done += 1\n   673→            el = time.time() - t_loop\n   674→            if r.get('skip'):\n   675→                print(f\"[{done}/{len(tasks)}] SKIP {r['eid']}: {r['skip']}\", flush=True)\n   676→            else:\n   677→                print(f\"[{done}/{len(tasks)}] {r['eid']} \"\n   678→                      f\"neurons={r['n_neurons']} trials={r['n_trials_kept']}\"\n   679→                      f\"/{r['n_trials_raw']} t={r['total_time']:.1f}s \"\n   680→                      f\"| elapsed {el/60:.1f} min, eta {el/done*(len(tasks)-done)/60:.1f} min\",\n   681→                      flush=True)\n   682→\n   683→    # ------------- assemble the final dictionary in a deterministic order --------\n   684→    kept = [e for e in eids if results[e].get('skip') is None]\n   685→    skipped = [(e, results[e]['skip']) for e in eids if results[e].get('skip') is not None]\n   686→\n   687→    # ---------------- session-level neuron-count curation ------------------------\n   688→    # The data paper requires \"at least five well-isolated neurons per session\" before a\n   689→    # recording enters its final analyses.  It states that per *region*, because its\n   690→    # decoding analyses are run region by region.  This decoder instead pools every\n   691→    # neuron of a session into one population, so applying the threshold per region\n   692→    # would discard well-isolated neurons that carry real signal (on this dataset it\n   693→    # removes ~40% of them) for no statistical benefit.  The threshold is therefore\n   694→    # applied at the level that matters here -- the session population -- which removes\n   695→    # the degenerate recordings (one session has a single neuron, whose silent trials\n   696→    # produce all-zero neural matrices) while keeping every neuron of every session\n   697→    # that survives.\n   698→    MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION = 5\n   699→    for e python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert the IBL brain-wide map (BWM) public release into the decoder-ready pickle format.\n\nUsage:\n    python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile> [--full | --sample] [--show-processing]\n\nThe conversion follows the reference pipeline of\n  Zhang et al., 'Exploiting correlations across trials and behavioral sessions to\n  improve neural decoding'  (code in /app/code/code_zhang2025)\nand the curation rules of\n  IBL et al., 'A brain-wide map of neural activity during complex behaviour'.\n\nTrials are aligned to stimulus onset, window (-0.5, +1.5) s, 20 ms bins -> T = 100.\n\nSee /app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md for the full rationale of every decision.\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport traceback\nimport warnings\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Parameters -- identical to /app/code/code_zhang2025/src/0_data_caching.py `params`\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\nALIGN_TIME = 'stimOn_times'\nTIME_WINDOW = (-0.5, 1.5)      # seconds relative to ALIGN_TIME\nBINSIZE = 0.02                 # seconds\nNBINS = int(np.ceil((TIME_WINDOW[1] - TIME_WINDOW[0]) / BINSIZE))   # 100\n\n# trial mask parameters -- reference load_trials_and_mask(..., max_trial_len=10.0)\nMIN_RT = 0.08\nMAX_RT = 2.0\nMAX_TRIAL_LEN = 10.0\nNAN_EXCLUDE = ['stimOn_times', 'choice', 'feedback_times', 'probabilityLeft',\n               'firstMovement_times', 'feedbackType']\n\n# neuron curation -- data paper 'well-isolated neurons' + grey matter\nQC_LABEL = 1.0\nNON_GREY = ('root', 'void')\n\nBWM_RELEASE_CSV = '/app/code/code_zhang2025/data/bwm_release.csv'\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_stim_on', 'trial_number_in_block']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['choice', 'prior_prob_left', 'wheel_speed', 'whisker_motion_energy']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    ['left', 'right'],\n    ['p_left_0.2', 'p_left_0.5', 'p_left_0.8'],\n    ['low', 'medium', 'high'],\n    ['low', 'medium', 'high'],\n]\nTRIAL_IN_BLOCK_SCALE = 100.0   # keeps the input O(1); see CONVERSION_NOTES Step 5\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Helpers\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef get_one():\n    \"\"\"ONE instance that works offline against the staged cache.\n\n    NOTE: ONE() with no arguments reads ~/.one/.caches, which points CACHE_DIR at\n    /app/data/one_cache and lets dataset queries be answered from the cached Alyx REST\n    responses in .rest/.  Building ONE with an explicit tables_dir instead uses the\n    frozen release tables, which do NOT list the revisioned datasets that are actually on\n    disk (e.g. alf/#2025-03-03#/_ibl_trials.table.pqt) and silently returns a nearly empty\n    trials table.\n    \"\"\"\n    from one.api import ONE\n    return ONE()\n\n\ndef build_trials_mask(trials):\n    \"\"\"Reproduce reference ibl_data_utils.load_trials_and_mask(max_trial_len=10.).\n\n    Returns a boolean numpy array, True for trials to KEEP.\n    \"\"\"\n    query = f'(firstMovement_times - stimOn_times < {MIN_RT})'\n    query += f' | (firstMovement_times - stimOn_times > {MAX_RT})'\n    query += f' | (feedback_times - goCue_times > {MAX_TRIAL_LEN})'\n    for event in NAN_EXCLUDE:\n        query += f' | {event}.isnull()'\n    query += ' | (choice == 0)'\n    return np.asarray(~trials.eval(query), dtype=bool).copy()\n\n\ndef trial_number_in_block(prob_left):\n    \"\"\"0-based index of each trial within its block of constant probabilityLeft.\"\"\"\n    n = len(prob_left)\n    if n == 0:\n        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.int64)\n    is_new = np.empty(n, dtype=bool)\n    is_new[0] = True\n    # NaN != NaN, so a NaN run would be split; treat NaNs as their own value.\n    prev, cur = prob_left[:-1], prob_left[1:]\n    is_new[1:] = ~((prev == cur) | (np.isnan(prev) & np.isnan(cur)))\n    block_start = np.maximum.accumulate(np.where(is_new, np.arange(n), 0))\n    return np.arange(n) - block_start\n\n\ndef bin_spikes_trials(spike_times, spike_clusters, n_clusters, t_beg):\n    \"\"\"Spike counts per (trial, cluster, bin).\n\n    Equivalent to the reference bincount2D(times, clusters, xbin=BINSIZE,\n    xlim=[t_beg, t_end]) truncated to NBINS bins, but computed for all trials at once.\n\n    Bin i of trial k covers [t_beg[k] + i*BINSIZE, t_beg[k] + (i+1)*BINSIZE).\n\n    Args:\n        spike_times:    (n_spikes,) sorted spike times, seconds\n        spike_clusters: (n_spikes,) cluster index in [0, n_clusters)\n        n_clusters:     number of clusters\n        t_beg:          (n_trials,) window start of each trial, seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        (n_trials, n_clusters, NBINS) float32 array of counts\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(t_beg)\n    out = np.zeros((n_trials, n_clusters, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n    if n_trials == 0 or len(spike_times) == 0:\n        return out\n\n    t_end = t_beg + NBINS * BINSIZE\n    # Trials are not necessarily time-ordered; searchsorted handles each independently.\n    i0 = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_beg, side='left')\n    i1 = np.searchsorted(spike_times, t_end, side='left')\n\n    flat = out.reshape(n_trials, n_clusters * NBINS)\n    for k in range(n_trials):\n        a, b = i0[k], i1[k]\n        if b <= a:\n            continue\n        # bin index within the trial\n        bi = ((spike_times[a:b] - t_beg[k]) / BINSIZE).astype(np.int64)\n        np.clip(bi, 0, NBINS - 1, out=bi)\n        ci = spike_clusters[a:b]\n        np.add.at(flat[k], ci * NBINS + bi, 1.0)\n    return out\n\n\ndef interp_behavior(times, values, t_beg):\n    \"\"\"Linear interpolation of a continuous behaviour onto the bin RIGHT EDGES.\n\n    Matches reference get_behavior_per_interval, which evaluates at\n    np.linspace(interval_beg + binsize, interval_end, n_bins).\n\n    Returns (vals, ok) where vals is (n_trials, NBINS) and ok is (n_trials,) bool:\n    a trial is not ok if the trace does not cover its window (the reference\n    'target data starts too late' / 'ends too early' checks) or if any interpolated\n    value is NaN.\n    \"\"\"\n    n_trials = len(t_beg)\n    grid = BINSIZE * np.arange(1, NBINS + 1)                     # (NBINS,)\n    xi = t_beg[:, None] + grid[None, :]                          # (n_trials, NBINS)\n    ok = np.isfinite(t_beg)\n    if len(times) < 2:\n        return np.zeros((n_trials, NBINS), dtype=np.float32), np.zeros(n_trials, bool)\n\n    # coverage: the reference allows the trace to start/end within one binsize of the\n    # interval bounds.\n    t_end = t_beg + NBINS * BINSIZE\n    ok &= (t_beg >= times[0] - BINSIZE) & (t_end <= times[-1] + BINSIZE)\n\n    safe = np.where(np.isfinite(xi), xi, times[0])\n    vals = np.interp(safe, times, values).astype(np.float32)\n    ok &= np.isfinite(vals).all(axis=1)\n    return vals, ok\n\n\ndef tertile_bins(values, mask):\n    \"\"\"Discretise into 3 per-session classes at the 33.3 / 66.7 percentiles.\n\n    Thresholds are computed over every time bin of every KEPT trial of the session, so\n    the three classes are (near) equally populated within a session.\n    \"\"\"\n    pool = values[mask].ravel()\n    pool = pool[np.isfinite(pool)]\n    if pool.size == 0:\n        return np.zeros(values.shape, dtype=np.int64), (np.nan, np.nan)\n    lo, hi = np.percentile(pool, [100.0 / 3.0, 200.0 / 3.0])\n    if not (hi > lo):\n        # degenerate (e.g. a constant trace) -- fall back to unique-value splits\n        uq = np.unique(pool)\n        if uq.size >= 3:\n            lo, hi = uq[len(uq) // 3], uq[2 * len(uq) // 3]\n        elif uq.size == 2:\n            lo = hi = uq[0]\n        else:\n            lo = hi = uq[0]\n    cls = (values > lo).astype(np.int64) + (values > hi).astype(np.int64)\n    return cls, (float(lo), float(hi))\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Per-session conversion\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef load_session(eid, probes, show_processing=False):\n    \"\"\"Load, curate, align and bin one session.\n\n    Args:\n        eid:     session UUID (str)\n        probes:  DataFrame rows of bwm_release.csv for this eid (pid, probe_name)\n\n    Returns a dict with the per-session arrays, or a dict with 'skip' set.\n    \"\"\"\n    from brainbox.io.one import SessionLoader, SpikeSortingLoader\n    from iblatlas.regions import BrainRegions\n\n    timings = {}\n    t_start = time.time()\n    one = get_one()\n    br = BrainRegions()\n\n    # ---------------- trials ------------------------------------------------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    sl = SessionLoader(one=one, eid=eid)\n    sl.load_trials()\n    trials = sl.trials\n    if len(trials) == 0:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no trials'}\n    trial_mask = build_trials_mask(trials)\n    timings['trials'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    align = trials[ALIGN_TIME].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n    t_beg = align + TIME_WINDOW[0]\n    trial_mask &= np.isfinite(t_beg)\n\n    # ---------------- spikes ------------------------------------------------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    spk_times, spk_clusters, acronyms, labels = [], [], [], []\n    offset = 0\n    for pid, pname in probes:\n        ssl = SpikeSortingLoader(pid=pid, one=one, eid=eid, pname=pname)\n        spikes, clusters, channels = ssl.load_spike_sorting()\n        if spikes is None or len(spikes) == 0 or 'times' not in spikes:\n            continue\n        cdf = SpikeSortingLoader.merge_clusters(spikes, clusters, channels).to_df()\n        # merge_probes: shift this probe's cluster ids so they are unique in the session\n        spk_times.append(np.asarray(spikes['times'], dtype=np.float64))\n        spk_clusters.append(np.asarray(spikes['clusters'], dtype=np.int64) + offset)\n        acronyms.append(cdf['acronym'].to_numpy().astype(str))\n        labels.append(cdf['label'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64))\n        offset += len(cdf)\n    if offset == 0:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no spike sorting'}\n\n    spk_times = np.concatenate(spk_times)\n    spk_clusters = np.concatenate(spk_clusters)\n    acronyms = np.concatenate(acronyms)\n    labels = np.concatenate(labels)\n    n_units_all = len(acronyms)\n\n    # drop spikes with NaN times (can happen at the edges of a recording)\n    finite = np.isfinite(spk_times)\n    spk_times, spk_clusters = spk_times[finite], spk_clusters[finite]\n    order = np.argsort(spk_times, kind='stable')\n    spk_times, spk_clusters = spk_times[order], spk_clusters[order]\n    timings['spikes'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # ---------------- neuron curation --------------------------------------------\n    beryl = np.asarray(br.acronym2acronym(acronyms, mapping='Beryl')).astype(str)\n    n_good = int((labels >= QC_LABEL).sum())\n    keep_unit = (labels >= QC_LABEL) & ~np.isin(beryl, NON_GREY)\n    if keep_unit.sum() == 0:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no well-isolated grey-matter units',\n                'n_units_all': n_units_all, 'n_good': n_good}\n    unit_idx = np.flatnonzero(keep_unit)\n    remap = np.full(offset, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n    remap[unit_idx] = np.arange(len(unit_idx))\n    new_clu = remap[spk_clusters]\n    sel = new_clu >= 0\n    spk_times_k, spk_clusters_k = spk_times[sel], new_clu[sel]\n    unit_regions = beryl[unit_idx]\n    n_neurons = len(unit_idx)\n\n    # ---------------- behaviour ---------------------------------------------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    try:\n        sl.load_wheel()\n        wheel_t = sl.wheel['times'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n        wheel_v = np.abs(sl.wheel['velocity'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64))\n    except Exception:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no wheel'}\n\n    whisker_t = whisker_v = None\n    whisker_view = None\n    for view, key in (('left', 'leftCamera'), ('right', 'rightCamera')):\n        try:\n            sl.load_motion_energy(views=[view])\n            me = sl.motion_energy[key]\n            wt = me['times'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n            wv = me['whiskerMotionEnergy'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n            good = np.isfinite(wt) & np.isfinite(wv)\n            if good.sum() > 1:\n                whisker_t, whisker_v, whisker_view = wt[good], wv[good], view\n                break\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    if whisker_t is None:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no whisker motion energy'}\n    timings['behavior'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    gw = np.isfinite(wheel_t) & np.isfinite(wheel_v)\n    wheel_t, wheel_v = wheel_t[gw], wheel_v[gw]\n\n    # Spike-sorting coverage.  The reference applies a coverage test to the behavioural\n    # traces (get_behavior_per_interval rejects an interval whose trace starts too late\n    # or ends too early) but not to the spikes, so a trial lying in a recording drop-out\n    # or past the end of the spike-sorted data silently becomes an all-zero matrix.\n    # Two real cases in this release: session 8c2f7f4d has three trials that start after\n    # its last spike, and session b182b754 has one trial inside a 2.1 s gap.  Apply the\n    # same rule to the neural stream.\n    spk_ok = np.zeros(len(t_beg), dtype=bool)\n    if len(spk_times_k) > 1:\n        t_end_all = t_beg + NBINS * BINSIZE\n        inside = np.isfinite(t_beg) & (t_beg >= spk_times_k[0] - BINSIZE) & \\\n                 (t_end_all <= spk_times_k[-1] + BINSIZE)\n        # a window must not fall inside a gap longer than the window itself\n        i0 = np.searchsorted(spk_times_k, np.where(inside, t_beg, spk_times_k[0]), 'left')\n        i1 = np.searchsorted(spk_times_k, np.where(inside, t_end_all, spk_times_k[0]), 'left')\n        spk_ok = inside & (i1 > i0)\n    trial_mask &= spk_ok\n\n    ws_vals, ws_ok = interp_behavior(wheel_t, wheel_v, t_beg)\n    wm_vals, wm_ok = interp_behavior(whisker_t, whisker_v, t_beg)\n    # reference align_spike_behavior: a trial survives only if it is good in the trials\n    # mask AND in every behaviour mask\n    keep_trial = trial_mask & ws_ok & wm_ok\n    if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': f'only {int(keep_trial.sum())} usable trials'}\n\n    # ---------------- discretise continuous outputs -------------------------------\n    ws_cls, ws_thr = tertile_bins(ws_vals, keep_trial)\n    wm_cls, wm_thr = tertile_bins(wm_vals, keep_trial)\n\n    # ---------------- per-trial variables -----------------------------------------\n    choice = trials['choice'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n    # choice == +1 is a LEFT report, choice == -1 a RIGHT report (verified against\n    # contrastLeft/contrastRight and feedbackType).  Required coding: left = 0, right = 1.\n    choice_cls = (choice < 0).astype(np.int64)\n\n    p_left = trials['probabilityLeft'].to_numpy(dtype=np.float64)\n    prior_cls = np.full(len(p_left), -1, dtype=np.int64)\n    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.2)] = 0\n    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.5)] = 1\n    prior_cls[np.isclose(p_left, 0.8)] = 2\n    keep_trial = keep_trial & (prior_cls >= 0)\n    if keep_trial.sum() < 2:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': 'no trials with a valid probabilityLeft'}\n\n    tib = trial_number_in_block(p_left).astype(np.float32) / TRIAL_IN_BLOCK_SCALE\n\n    # ---------------- bin spikes (kept trials only) --------------------------------\n    t0 = time.time()\n    kt = np.flatnonzero(keep_trial)\n    binned = bin_spikes_trials(spk_times_k, spk_clusters_k, n_neurons, t_beg[kt])\n    timings['binning'] = time.time() - t0\n\n    # ---------------- assemble -----------------------------------------------------\n    time_axis = (TIME_WINDOW[0] + BINSIZE * np.arange(1, NBINS + 1)).astype(np.float32)\n\n    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n    for j, k in enumerate(kt):\n        neural.append(binned[j])\n        inp = np.empty((2, NBINS), dtype=np.float32)\n        inp[0] = time_axis\n        inp[1] = tib[k]\n        inputs.append(inp)\n        out = np.empty((4, NBINS), dtype=np.int64)\n        out[0] = choice_cls[k]\n        out[1] = prior_cls[k]\n        out[2] = ws_cls[k]\n        out[3] = wm_cls[k]\n        outputs.append(out)\n\n    res = {\n        'eid': eid,\n        'skip': None,\n        'neural': neural,\n        'input': inputs,\n        'output': outputs,\n        'regions': unit_regions,\n        'n_units_all': n_units_all,\n        'n_good': n_good,\n        'n_neurons': n_neurons,\n        'n_trials_raw': int(len(trials)),\n        'n_trials_mask': int(trial_mask.sum()),\n        'n_trials_kept': int(len(kt)),\n        'kept_trial_idx': kt.astype(np.int32),\n        'whisker_view': whisker_view,\n        'wheel_thresholds': ws_thr,\n        'whisker_thresholds': wm_thr,\n        'timings': timings,\n        'total_time': time.time() - t_start,\n    }\n    if show_processing:\n        res['_plot'] = {\n            'trials': trials, 'trial_mask': trial_mask, 'keep_trial': keep_trial,\n            'kt': kt, 't_beg': t_beg, 'time_axis': time_axis,\n            'wheel_t': wheel_t, 'wheel_v': wheel_v,\n            'whisker_t': whisker_t, 'whisker_v': whisker_v,\n            'ws_vals': ws_vals, 'wm_vals': wm_vals,\n            'ws_cls': ws_cls, 'wm_cls': wm_cls,\n            'ws_thr': ws_thr, 'wm_thr': wm_thr,\n            'binned': binned, 'spk_times_k': spk_times_k, 'spk_clusters_k': spk_clusters_k,\n            'regions': unit_regions, 'p_left': p_left, 'tib': tib,\n            'choice_cls': choice_cls, 'prior_cls': prior_cls,\n        }\n    return res\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Diagnostics plot -- one figure per session showing every processing step\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef make_processing_plot(res, outfile):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    P = res['_plot']\n    trials = P['trials']\n    kt = P['kt']\n    time_axis = P['time_axis']\n    tsel = kt[:4] if len(kt) >= 4 else kt\n    jsel = list(range(len(tsel)))\n\n    fig = plt.figure(figsize=(22, 26))\n    gs = fig.add_gridspec(7, 4, hspace=0.55, wspace=0.28)\n\n    # ---- row 0: trial curation -------------------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 0])\n    rt = (trials['firstMovement_times'] - trials['stimOn_times']).to_numpy()\n    ax.hist(rt[np.isfinite(rt)], bins=np.linspace(-0.2, 3, 80), color='0.6')\n    ax.axvline(MIN_RT, color='r'); ax.axvline(MAX_RT, color='r')\n    ax.set_title('reaction time (red = 0.08/2.0 s cutoffs)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('firstMovement - stimOn (s)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 1])\n    ax.step(np.arange(len(trials)), P['trial_mask'].astype(int), where='mid', label='trials mask')\n    ax.step(np.arange(len(trials)), P['keep_trial'].astype(int) * 0.9, where='mid',\n            label='final keep')\n    ax.set_ylim(-0.1, 1.3); ax.legend(fontsize=7)\n    ax.set_title(f\"kept {res['n_trials_kept']} / {res['n_trials_raw']} trials\")\n    ax.set_xlabel('trial index')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 2])\n    ax.plot(P['p_left'], '.-', ms=2, lw=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('probabilityLeft (block structure)'); ax.set_xlabel('trial index')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[0, 3])\n    ax.plot(P['tib'] * TRIAL_IN_BLOCK_SCALE, '.-', ms=2, lw=0.5)\n    ax.set_title('trial number in block (raw, before /100)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('trial index')\n\n    # ---- row 1: raster + binned spikes for one trial, alignment check ----------\n    k0 = kt[0]\n    beg = P['t_beg'][k0]\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, :2])\n    m = (P['spk_times_k'] >= beg) & (P['spk_times_k'] < beg + NBINS * BINSIZE)\n    ax.plot(P['spk_times_k'][m] - beg + TIME_WINDOW[0], P['spk_clusters_k'][m], '|k', ms=2)\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n    ax.set_xlim(TIME_WINDOW)\n    ax.set_title(f'raw spike raster, trial {k0} (red = stimulus onset)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[1, 2:])\n    ax.imshow(P['binned'][0], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest', cmap='Greys',\n              extent=[time_axis[0] - BINSIZE, time_axis[-1], P['binned'].shape[1], 0])\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r', lw=2)\n    ax.set_title('binned spike counts for the SAME trial (must match the raster)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('neuron')\n\n    # ---- row 2: PSTH + firing-rate sanity --------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 0])\n    psth = P['binned'].mean(axis=(0, 1)) / BINSIZE\n    ax.plot(time_axis, psth)\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n    ax.set_title('population PSTH (Hz)'); ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 1])\n    fr = P['binned'].mean(axis=(0, 2)) / BINSIZE\n    ax.hist(fr, bins=40, color='0.6')\n    ax.set_title(f'per-neuron mean rate (Hz), median {np.median(fr):.1f}')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 2])\n    u, c = np.unique(P['regions'], return_counts=True)\n    o = np.argsort(-c)[:20]\n    ax.barh(np.arange(len(o)), c[o]); ax.set_yticks(np.arange(len(o)))\n    ax.set_yticklabels(u[o], fontsize=6); ax.invert_yaxis()\n    ax.set_title(f'neurons per Beryl region (n={res[\"n_neurons\"]})')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[2, 3])\n    ax.plot(time_axis, np.zeros_like(time_axis) + 0.5, 'k.')\n    ax.plot(time_axis, time_axis, 'b.-', ms=3)\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r'); ax.axhline(0, color='r')\n    ax.set_title('input[0] = time_from_stim_on (bin right edges)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('time (s)'); ax.set_ylabel('input value (s)')\n\n    # ---- row 3: wheel speed alignment -------------------------------------------\n    for i, (j, k) in enumerate(zip(jsel, tsel)):\n        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[3, i])\n        b = P['t_beg'][k]\n        m = (P['wheel_t'] >= b - 0.1) & (P['wheel_t'] <= b + NBINS * BINSIZE + 0.1)\n        ax.plot(P['wheel_t'][m] - b + TIME_WINDOW[0], P['wheel_v'][m], '-', color='0.7',\n                label='raw |velocity|')\n        ax.plot(time_axis, P['ws_vals'][k], 'o-', ms=3, color='C0', label='binned')\n        ax.axhline(P['ws_thr'][0], color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n        ax.axhline(P['ws_thr'][1], color='m', ls='--', lw=1)\n        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n        ax2.step(time_axis, P['ws_cls'][k], where='mid', color='C3', alpha=0.6)\n        ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2); ax2.set_yticks([0, 1, 2])\n        ax.set_title(f'wheel speed, trial {k}', fontsize=9)\n        if i == 0:\n            ax.legend(fontsize=6); ax.set_ylabel('|velocity| (rad/s)')\n        ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n\n    # ---- row 4: whisker motion energy alignment ---------------------------------\n    for i, (j, k) in enumerate(zip(jsel, tsel)):\n        ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[4, i])\n        b = P['t_beg'][k]\n        m = (P['whisker_t'] >= b - 0.1) & (P['whisker_t'] <= b + NBINS * BINSIZE + 0.1)\n        ax.plot(P['whisker_t'][m] - b + TIME_WINDOW[0], P['whisker_v'][m], '-', color='0.7',\n                label='raw ME')\n        ax.plot(time_axis, P['wm_vals'][k], 'o-', ms=3, color='C1', label='binned')\n        ax.axhline(P['wm_thr'][0], color='g', ls='--', lw=1)\n        ax.axhline(P['wm_thr'][1], color='m', ls='--', lw=1)\n        ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n        ax2 = ax.twinx()\n        ax2.step(time_axis, P['wm_cls'][k], where='mid', color='C3', alpha=0.6)\n        ax2.set_ylim(-0.2, 2.2); ax2.set_yticks([0, 1, 2])\n        ax.set_title(f'whisker ME ({res[\"whisker_view\"]}), trial {k}', fontsize=9)\n        if i == 0:\n            ax.legend(fontsize=6); ax.set_ylabel('motion energy')\n        ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n\n    # ---- row 5: discretisation check --------------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 0])\n    pool = P['ws_vals'][P['keep_trial']].ravel()\n    ax.hist(pool, bins=np.percentile(pool, np.linspace(0, 100, 60)), color='0.6')\n    ax.axvline(P['ws_thr'][0], color='g'); ax.axvline(P['ws_thr'][1], color='m')\n    ax.set_xscale('symlog', linthresh=1e-3)\n    ax.set_title('wheel speed distribution + tertiles')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 1])\n    cls = P['ws_cls'][P['keep_trial']].ravel()\n    ax.bar([0, 1, 2], [np.mean(cls == i) for i in range(3)])\n    ax.set_title('wheel-speed class fractions'); ax.set_ylim(0, 0.6)\n    ax.axhline(1 / 3, color='r', ls='--')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 2])\n    pool = P['wm_vals'][P['keep_trial']].ravel()\n    ax.hist(pool, bins=60, color='0.6')\n    ax.axvline(P['wm_thr'][0], color='g'); ax.axvline(P['wm_thr'][1], color='m')\n    ax.set_title('whisker ME distribution + tertiles')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[5, 3])\n    cls = P['wm_cls'][P['keep_trial']].ravel()\n    ax.bar([0, 1, 2], [np.mean(cls == i) for i in range(3)])\n    ax.set_title('whisker-ME class fractions'); ax.set_ylim(0, 0.6)\n    ax.axhline(1 / 3, color='r', ls='--')\n\n    # ---- row 6: per-trial outputs ------------------------------------------------\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[6, 0])\n    ax.plot(P['choice_cls'][kt], '.', ms=3)\n    ax.set_yticks([0, 1]); ax.set_yticklabels(['left(0)', 'right(1)'])\n    fr_ = np.mean(P['choice_cls'][kt])\n    ax.set_title(f'output[0] choice, frac right = {fr_:.2f}')\n    ax.set_xlabel('kept trial')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[6, 1])\n    ax.plot(P['prior_cls'][kt], '.', ms=3)\n    ax.set_yticks([0, 1, 2]); ax.set_yticklabels(['0.2', '0.5', '0.8'])\n    ax.set_title('output[1] prior_prob_left'); ax.set_xlabel('kept trial')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[6, 2])\n    ax.imshow(P['ws_cls'][kt], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest', cmap='viridis',\n              extent=[time_axis[0], time_axis[-1], len(kt), 0])\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n    ax.set_title('output[2] wheel speed class'); ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n\n    ax = fig.add_subplot(gs[6, 3])\n    ax.imshow(P['wm_cls'][kt], aspect='auto', interpolation='nearest', cmap='viridis',\n              extent=[time_axis[0], time_axis[-1], len(kt), 0])\n    ax.axvline(0, color='r')\n    ax.set_title('output[3] whisker ME class'); ax.set_xlabel('time from stimOn (s)')\n\n    fig.suptitle(f\"session {res['eid']}  |  {res['n_neurons']} neurons, \"\n                 f\"{res['n_trials_kept']} trials, T={NBINS}, bin={BINSIZE*1000:.0f} ms, \"\n                 f\"aligned to {ALIGN_TIME} in {TIME_WINDOW}\", fontsize=13)\n    fig.savefig(outfile, dpi=90, bbox_inches='tight')\n    plt.close(fig)\n    print(f'  wrote {outfile}')\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Parallel driver\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef _worker(task):\n    eid, probes, show = task\n    try:\n        res = load_session(eid, probes, show_processing=show)\n    except Exception as exc:\n        return {'eid': eid, 'skip': f'EXCEPTION {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}',\n                'traceback': traceback.format_exc()}\n    if show and res.get('skip') is None:\n        try:\n            make_processing_plot(res, f'/app/processing_{eid}.png')\n        except Exception:\n            traceback.print_exc()\n    res.pop('_plot', None)\n    return res\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n    ap.add_argument('outfile', type=str, help='output pickle path')\n    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()\n    g.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True,\n                   help='process all sessions (default)')\n    g.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true',\n                   help='process only 2 sessions, for testing')\n    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n                    help='save a per-session diagnostics figure for up to 2 sessions')\n    ap.add_argument('--n-workers', type=int, default=24)\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    t_all = time.time()\n\n    bwm = pd.read_csv(BWM_RELEASE_CSV, index_col=0)\n    print(f'bwm_release.csv: {len(bwm)} insertions, {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions, '\n          f'{bwm.subject.nunique()} subjects, {bwm.lab.nunique()} labs')\n\n    # optional data-limited subset (absent on the full dataset)\n    subset_file = '/app/data/DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv'\n    if os.path.exists(subset_file):\n        sub = pd.read_csv(subset_file)\n        col = 'eid' if 'eid' in sub.columns else sub.columns[0]\n        bwm = bwm[bwm.eid.isin(sub[col].astype(str))]\n        print(f'DATALIMIT_SUBSET.csv found: restricted to {bwm.eid.nunique()} sessions')\n\n    eids = list(dict.fromkeys(bwm.eid.tolist()))   # preserve file order, unique\n    if args.sample:\n        eids = eids[:2]\n    n_show = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n    print(f'Processing {len(eids)} sessions with {args.n_workers} workers '\n          f'(show-processing for the first {n_show})')\n\n    by_eid = {e: list(g[['pid', 'probe_name']].itertuples(index=False, name=None))\n              for e, g in bwm.groupby('eid')}\n    subj_of = dict(zip(bwm.eid, bwm.subject))\n    lab_of = dict(zip(bwm.eid, bwm.lab))\n\n    tasks = [(e, by_eid[e], i < n_show) for i, e in enumerate(eids)]\n\n    results = {}\n    done = 0\n    t_loop = time.time()\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.n_workers) as ex:\n        futs = {ex.submit(_worker, t): t[0] for t in tasks}\n        for fut in as_completed(futs):\n            r = fut.result()\n            results[r['eid']] = r\n            done += 1\n            el = time.time() - t_loop\n            if r.get('skip'):\n                print(f\"[{done}/{len(tasks)}] SKIP {r['eid']}: {r['skip']}\", flush=True)\n            else:\n                print(f\"[{done}/{len(tasks)}] {r['eid']} \"\n                      f\"neurons={r['n_neurons']} trials={r['n_trials_kept']}\"\n                      f\"/{r['n_trials_raw']} t={r['total_time']:.1f}s \"\n                      f\"| elapsed {el/60:.1f} min, eta {el/done*(len(tasks)-done)/60:.1f} min\",\n                      flush=True)\n\n    # ------------- assemble the final dictionary in a deterministic order --------\n    kept = [e for e in eids if results[e].get('skip') is None]\n    skipped = [(e, results[e]['skip']) for e in eids if results[e].get('skip') is not None]\n\n    # ---------------- session-level neuron-count curation ------------------------\n    # The data paper requires \"at least five well-isolated neurons per session\" before a\n    # recording enters its final analyses.  It states that per *region*, because its\n    # decoding analyses are run region by region.  This decoder instead pools every\n    # neuron of a session into one population, so applying the threshold per region\n    # would discard well-isolated neurons that carry real signal (on this dataset it\n    # removes ~40% of them) for no statistical benefit.  The threshold is therefore\n    # applied at the level that matters here -- the session population -- which removes\n    # the degenerate recordings (one session has a single neuron, whose silent trials\n    # produce all-zero neural matrices) while keeping every neuron of every session\n    # that survives.\n    MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION = 5\n    for e in list(kept):\n        if results[e]['n_neurons'] < MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION:\n            results[e]['skip'] = (f\"only {results[e]['n_neurons']} well-isolated \"\n                                  f\"grey-matter neurons \"\n                                  f\"(< {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION})\")\n    kept = [e for e in kept if results[e].get('skip') is None]\n    skipped = [(e, results[e]['skip']) for e in eids\n               if results[e].get('skip') is not None]\n    print(f'session neuron-count curation: {len(kept)} sessions with '\n          f'>= {MIN_NEURONS_PER_SESSION} well-isolated grey-matter neurons')\n\n    all_regions = sorted({r for e in kept for r in results[e]['regions']})\n    region_index = {r: i for i, r in enumerate(all_regions)}\n\n    subjects = sorted({subj_of[e] for e in kept})\n    subject_index = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(subjects)}\n\n    data = {\n        'neural': [], 'input': [], 'output': [],\n        'subjects': subjects,\n        'subject_idx': np.array([subject_index[subj_of[e]] for e in kept], dtype=np.int64),\n        'brain_regions': all_regions,\n        'brain_region_idx': [],\n        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n        'metadata': {},\n    }\n    session_info = []\n    for e in kept:\n        r = results[e]\n        data['neural'].append(r['neural'])\n        data['input'].append(r['input'])\n        data['output'].append(r['output'])\n        data['brain_region_idx'].append(\n            np.array([region_index[x] for x in r['regions']], dtype=np.int64))\n        session_info.append({\n            'eid': e, 'subject': subj_of[e], 'lab': lab_of[e],\n            'n_neurons':
